# Regression ledger

Every bug found and fixed in IsoKron's tools, from each repository's own `REGRESSIONS.md`.
Generated by [`scripts/ledger.mjs`](../scripts/ledger.mjs); do not edit by hand. The same records,
one per row, are in [`ledger/ledger.json`](../ledger/ledger.json).

**1058 rows** across 5 repositories.

| Tool | Rows | Critical | High | Medium | Low | No severity | Unresolved |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| [tool-qa](https://github.com/isokron-ai/tool-qa) | 563 | 0 | 117 | 257 | 180 | 9 | 19 |
| [mcp-check](https://github.com/isokron-ai/mcp-check) | 275 | 0 | 81 | 107 | 60 | 27 | 8 |
| [mcp-surface-corpus](https://github.com/isokron-ai/mcp-surface-corpus) | 114 | 0 | 14 | 13 | 1 | 86 | 0 |
| [mcp-writecheck-action](https://github.com/isokron-ai/mcp-writecheck-action) | 59 | 0 | 16 | 25 | 10 | 8 | 0 |
| [mcp-write-safety](https://github.com/isokron-ai/mcp-write-safety) | 47 | 0 | 16 | 16 | 15 | 0 | 8 |

Found by: unknown 539, review 254, design 107, CI 46, self-test 29, mutation 22, design of the publishing path 10, loop gates research 9, building lane-drain 8, chaos suite 7, the same 5, live run 4, design of the mcpb kind 3, design check 2, the brief 2, the first public snapshot 2, the same dry run 2, building release-diff 1, design of the uv bundle runtime 1, the existing collect and pump tests failing while this was built 1, the first local run of the harness 1, the fixture test before first use 1, the unit test for the install manifest 1, the unit tests written for the judge 1.

## Cycle time

Hours from a row being found (the earlier of its first commit in `REGRESSIONS.md` and its fix pull request being opened) to the fix merging, and from the merge to the first release that contains it. A row with no date yet, or with a gap that runs backwards, is left out of that figure and counted ("Left out" counts rows, once each). Medians and p90 (nearest rank) are over the rows counted.

| Tool | Rows | Found → merged (rows) | Median | p90 | Merged → released (rows) | Median | p90 | Merged, not released | No date | Left out |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| All tools | 1058 | 551 | 0.6 | 2.0 | 0 | – | – | 1027 | 507 | 0 |
| [tool-qa](https://github.com/isokron-ai/tool-qa) | 563 | 544 | 0.6 | 2.0 | 0 | – | – | 544 | 19 | 0 |
| [mcp-check](https://github.com/isokron-ai/mcp-check) | 275 | 7 | 1.0 | 1.0 | 0 | – | – | 268 | 268 | 0 |
| [mcp-surface-corpus](https://github.com/isokron-ai/mcp-surface-corpus) | 114 | 0 | – | – | 0 | – | – | 114 | 114 | 0 |
| [mcp-writecheck-action](https://github.com/isokron-ai/mcp-writecheck-action) | 59 | 0 | – | – | 0 | – | – | 59 | 59 | 0 |
| [mcp-write-safety](https://github.com/isokron-ai/mcp-write-safety) | 47 | 0 | – | – | 0 | – | – | 42 | 47 | 0 |

## Column shapes

Every ledger is read into one shape: `ID | What it was | Pinned by | Fix`, with tests written `file` › `name`.

- isokron-ai/tool-qa: already in that shape.
- isokron-ai/mcp-check: normalised: some test references are in no known form (kept as text).
- isokron-ai/mcp-surface-corpus: normalised: test references written `file` `name` read as `file` › `name`.
- isokron-ai/mcp-writecheck-action: already in that shape.
- isokron-ai/mcp-write-safety: already in that shape.

| R-SNAP-03 | medium | A snapshot that does not read back intact is worse than none, since it is only found out during a restore. | design | `snapshot/test/snapshot.test.mjs` › R-SNAP-03: verifySnapshot accepts the bytes the manifest describes and refuses a changed byte, a wrong size, a wrong name or plaintext<br>`snapshot/test/snapshot.test.mjs` › R-SNAP-03: an upload that does not read back intact is deleted and the run fails | [8200407](https://github.com/isokron-ai/tool-qa/commit/8200407) | [#24](https://github.com/isokron-ai/tool-qa/pull/24) | 2026-11-24 | 2026-11-24 | 2026-11-24 |  |
| R-SNAP-04 | medium | The snapshot runs outside the pump's concurrency group, so the pump can replace the asset while it is being copied. | design | `snapshot/test/snapshot.test.mjs` › R-SNAP-04: the pump pushing during the copy is detected and the copy is retried once<br>`snapshot/test/snapshot.test.mjs` › R-SNAP-04: bytes that do not match the label, twice, fail the run and upload nothing<br>`snapshot/test/snapshot.test.mjs` › R-SNAP-04: an interrupted pump push (temporary name only) is copied like the pump would read it<br>`snapshot/test/snapshot.test.mjs` › R-SNAP-04: a plaintext or unlabelled state asset is never copied | [8200407](https://github.com/isokron-ai/tool-qa/commit/8200407) | [#24](https://github.com/isokron-ai/tool-qa/pull/24) | 2026-11-24 | 2026-11-24 | 2026-11-24 |  |

| R-SNAP-07 | high | There was no tested way to put a snapshot back. | design | `snapshot/test/restore.test.mjs` › R-SNAP-07: inspectDatabase reports integrity, schema version, the fence and row counts, subjects and results first<br>`snapshot/test/restore.test.mjs` › R-SNAP-07: the drill verifies, decrypts and checks a snapshot with the age identity, and leaves no plaintext behind<br>`snapshot/test/restore.test.mjs` › R-SNAP-07: a snapshot whose bytes differ from the manifest is refused before it is decrypted<br>`snapshot/test/restore.test.mjs` › R-SNAP-07: a snapshot that decrypts to a damaged database fails the drill<br>`snapshot/test/restore.test.mjs` › R-SNAP-07: --apply needs the loop stopped: switched off, pump lane off, or paused for at least 10 more minutes<br>`snapshot/test/restore.test.mjs` › R-SNAP-07: --apply refuses while the loop runs or a pump run is active, and changes nothing<br>`snapshot/test/restore.test.mjs` › R-SNAP-07: --apply keeps the live state as a snapshot first, then puts the chosen snapshot back byte for byte<br>`snapshot/test/restore.test.mjs` › R-SNAP-07: --apply replaces a live asset that cannot be copied, and keeps it renamed<br>`snapshot/test/restore.test.mjs` › R-SNAP-07: --apply leaves the live state alone if it changes during the restore<br>`snapshot/test/restore.test.mjs` › R-SNAP-07: a snapshot that opens but fails PRAGMA integrity_check fails the drill with report.ok false | [8200407](https://github.com/isokron-ai/tool-qa/commit/8200407), [10c4576](https://github.com/isokron-ai/tool-qa/commit/10c4576) | [#24](https://github.com/isokron-ai/tool-qa/pull/24) | 2026-11-24 | 2026-11-24 | 2026-11-24 |  |
